FUNCTION 'web_search_tool'
## Andrej Karpathy — biography and current role

**Official bio:** Andrej Karpathy is an AI researcher and educator. He earned a PhD in computer science from Stanford, advised by Fei-Fei Li, and was the architect and primary instructor for Stanford’s original deep-learning course, **CS231n**. He was a founding member and research scientist at OpenAI (2015–2017), then Tesla’s Director of AI (2017–2022), leading the computer-vision work for Autopilot. He returned to OpenAI in 2023–2024 to build a team focused on mid-training and synthetic-data generation, and also founded the AI-education venture Eureka Labs. ([karpathy.ai](https://karpathy.ai/))

**Current role (as of November 18, 2026):** Karpathy joined **Anthropic** in May 2026 as part of its **pretraining team**. He was reported to be building a team that uses Claude to accelerate pretraining research. ([cnbc.com](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/19/anthropic-hires-openai-cofounder-andrej-karpathy-former-tesla-ai-lead.html))

## Contact

- **Publicly listed email:** `andrej.karpathy@gmail.com`  
  This appears in a résumé hosted on his Stanford personal web space, but that résumé is old; there is no confirmation that the address is currently monitored. ([cs.stanford.edu](https://cs.stanford.edu/~karpathy/Andrej_Karpathy_Resume.pdf))
- **Official site:** Karpathy’s personal site directs readers to his X/Twitter and GitHub for the latest updates; it does not list a current dedicated contact address. ([karpathy.ai](https://karpathy.ai/))
- **Eureka Labs:** Its official site offers newsletter, X, GitHub, and Discord channels rather than a published business email. ([eurekalabs.ai](https://eurekalabs.ai/))

You manage records in a User Service on behalf of the operator.\n\n## Tools\n- search_users: find users by name, surname, email or gender (all filters optional).\n- get_user_by_id: fetch the full record of a user by numeric ID.\n- add_user: create a user (required: name, surname, email, about_me).\n- update_user: change fields of an existing user by ID.\n- delete_users: delete a user by ID (irreversible).\n- web_search_tool: search the web for public information.\n\n## Behaviour\n- Stay within the user-management domain; politely decline unrelated requests.\n- Use tools instead of guessing. Never invent user data or IDs.\n- Creating a user: if the request names a (public) person with few details, first use web_search_tool to\n  gather facts (e.g. who they are, company, a short bio for `about_me`), then call add_user.
